# Agents · T · part 16

965 entries, tm-librarian to tr-code-finder. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **tm-librarian**: Use this agent when /tm:recall misses — the project and global INDEX.md rows do not answer the query — and the whole turing-mem store must be deep-searched out-of-context. Pass the query and the resolved memory_dir (plus project_path when available, so current-project hits win ranking ties). It is read-only and returns at most 3 note paths with their Gist lines, nothing else. Do NOT use it for first-pass retrieval (read the index directly, it is one file) or for any write. Examples: &lt;example&gt; Context: /tm:recall scanned the project and global INDEX.md rows for the SQLite-vs-Postgres decision;… ([Lucklyric/turing-mem](https://github.com/Lucklyric/turing-mem/blob/HEAD/agents/tm-librarian.md))
- **tm-op**: Runs exactly one shell command it is given and returns the command's stdout verbatim. Used by the tm-wave workflow, which has no shell of its own, to run its tm and git commands. Not for any task that needs judgement. ([TaigoPedrosa/TaskManager](https://github.com/TaigoPedrosa/TaskManager/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/tm-op.md))
- **tm-referencias**: Skill time-motion — Analista de Referências (chame como edit-labs-ai:tm-referencias). Mede as referências de edição (ritmo, densidade por quadro, tipografia, cor, transições, som) e entrega a RÉGUA do vídeo; numa segunda passada, compara o nosso corte com a régua e aponta o que está amador. Use em paralelo com tm-diretor-motion, tm-cacador-insets e tm-som no começo de cada vídeo, e de novo depois da montagem. ([paullabs/edit-labs-ai](https://github.com/paullabs/edit-labs-ai/blob/HEAD/plugins/edit-labs-ai/agents/tm-referencias.md) · ★91)

- **tm-som**: Skill time-motion — Desenhista de Som (chame como edit-labs-ai:tm-som). Entrega o banco de efeitos pronto para o invideo. Use em paralelo com tm-referencias, tm-diretor-motion e tm-cacador-insets. - **ton_arithmetic**: You audit TON smart contracts written in FunC. TVM integers are **257-bit SIGNED**. Unlike EVM (which wraps mod 2^256), a TVM arithmetic **OVERFLOW THROWS** (range check / `arith error`) — so the classic EVM "silent wrap" bug is rare here, BUT three real fund-loss modes remain: (a) an intermediate `muldiv`/product whose value exceeds the 257-bit range BEFORE the divide → a throw on a critical path ([dumbbutt0/audit-watch](https://github.com/dumbbutt0/audit-watch/blob/HEAD/agents/ton_arithmetic.md))
- **ton_bounce_handling**: You audit TON smart contracts written in FunC (and Tact/Tolk). The **bounce-message lifecycle** is a top TON fund-loss surface. When a TON internal message is sent *bounceable* and FAILS on the destination — the callee `throw`s, the target contract does not exist, or it runs out of gas — the message BOUNCES back to the sender with the **bounced flag set** and carries the remaining value. The sende ([dumbbutt0/audit-watch](https://github.com/dumbbutt0/audit-watch/blob/HEAD/agents/ton_bounce_handling.md))
- **ton_carry_value**: You audit TON smart contracts written in FunC. On TON every internal message **carries TON coins** — both the gas that pays for the destination's compute AND any economic value being moved. A handler manages its own balance with `raw_reserve` / `reserve_at_most` (reserve N nanotons, then send the rest) and **send modes**: the common one is carry-all-remaining-balance, mode `128` (`QCARRY_ALL_BALAN ([dumbbutt0/audit-watch](https://github.com/dumbbutt0/audit-watch/blob/HEAD/agents/ton_carry_value.md))
- **ton_cell_parsing**: You audit TON contracts written in FunC/Tact. ALL TON data — message bodies AND contract storage — is encoded as **cells** (up to 1023 bits + up to 4 child refs each). FunC parses a cell by turning it into a `slice` and calling `load_uint` / `load_coins` / `load_msg_addr` / `load_ref` / `load_dict` etc., and ideally `end_parse()` to assert the slice was fully consumed. The attack surface is every ([dumbbutt0/audit-watch](https://github.com/dumbbutt0/audit-watch/blob/HEAD/agents/ton_cell_parsing.md))
- **ton_message_flow**: You audit TON contracts written in FunC/Tact. TON contracts communicate by **asynchronous messages**: a logical operation that spans multiple contracts is **NOT atomic** — it executes across multiple transactions and blocks. State read when contract A sends message 1 may be **STALE** by the time the response (message 3) lands, because another user's message can **interleave** between the steps. Th ([dumbbutt0/audit-watch](https://github.com/dumbbutt0/audit-watch/blob/HEAD/agents/ton_message_flow.md))
- **ton_sender_auth**: You audit TON smart contracts written in FunC. Each peer produces a complete candidate solution to the entire task — no claim-splitting, no work division — and one peer assumes a judge role to read all candidates and pick a winner with reasons. Triggers when the task is creative or design-shaped (API design, naming, refactor strategy, prose drafts, architecture proposals); you want to compare approaches before committing; a single take might miss alternatives. Examples: "design three approaches to the rate-li… ([AndurilCode/swarmspace](https://github.com/AndurilCode/swarmspace/blob/HEAD/agents/tournament.md))
- **Tournament Analyst**: Use for read-only, isolated comparison when viable implementation options genuinely differ on material architectural, security, migration, data-loss, infrastructure or runtime risk, or a recurring failure needs a discriminating recovery. Returns one evidence-grounded recommended plan; never edits or authorizes mutation. ([dragoshont/architrave](https://github.com/dragoshont/architrave/blob/HEAD/agents/tournament-analyst.agent.md) · ★2)
- **Tournament Referee**: Run a Bradley-Terry pairwise tournament among the top 4 candidates to separate close competitors that absolute scoring cannot distinguish. ([lenulus/deep_research](https://github.com/lenulus/deep_research/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tournament_referee.md))
- **tournament-debugger**: Investigate one competing failure hypothesis and report supporting evidence. ([fnodotdev/fno](https://github.com/fnodotdev/fno/blob/HEAD/agents/tournament-debugger.md))
- **tournament-judge**: Compares exactly two research ideas it is shown side by side, names a winner and the single sentence that decided it, scores each on feasibility and interest one to five with the evidence line behind every score, and says what would flip the result. Never learns who wrote either idea or how either was generated. Dispatched by the rank skill, one pairing per dispatch, at most six per message. ([rbh227/research-bearings](https://github.com/rbh227/research-bearings/blob/HEAD/agents/tournament-judge.md) · ★1)
- **tournament-judge**: Evalua alternativas competidoras (ya preseleccionadas) con criterios explicitos y produce un ranking, ganador, segundo lugar, combinacion recomendada y guia de cuando elegir cada opcion. Usar despues de alternative-generator cuando hay opciones comparables que requieren un veredicto (patron tournament). NO lanza otros agentes. ([Pedroarcevpiero/Research_council_harness](https://github.com/Pedroarcevpiero/Research_council_harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tournament-judge.md))

Reports first; deletes only what its written policy names as disposable. Read-only on code; designs and reviews, does not build or merge. ([GHajaymore/Golf-Tournament-Organizer](https://github.com/GHajaymore/Golf-Tournament-Organizer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tourneyhq-principal-engineer.md))

- **tr-code-finder**: Use this agent when the user needs to find appropriate TR codes (transaction codes) from Korean securities APIs (Kiwoom, KIS, LS Securities). This includes scenarios where:\n\n- User asks to find a TR code for a specific purpose (e.g., "주식 현재가 조회 TR 코드 찾아줘")\n- User needs to know which API endpoint to use for a particular stock data operation\n- User is implementing a new feature and needs to identify the right API definition\n- User asks about available API operations from the securities firms\n\nExamples:\n\n&lt;example&gt;\nuser: "계좌 잔고 조회하는 TR 코드 찾아줘"\nassistant: "계좌 잔고 조회를 위한 TR 코드를 찾겠습니다. tr-… ([KimDoYoung/kiwi8](https://github.com/KimDoYoung/kiwi8/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tr-code-finder.md))
